%% u0014:0-6, u0015:0-88, u0016:0-81, u0017:0-149, u0018:0-96, u0019:0-79, u0020:0-145 %%
DM: As we, begin our adventure tonight, a camera pans over a group of five adventurers on the road, surrounded by a pair of dead wyverns in the midst of an escalating confrontation, the rolling grasslands [garbled] stretching all directions, forest trees dotting land, the heat of the morning vanishing in the afternoon. Our adventurers have traveled for the past five days towards Darba following rumors of treasure. On this road, the rumors of danger grew: bandits in the Copper Hills, a dragon. The adventurers avoided battle with the bandits rumored to be making trouble in these parts and proceeded cautiously, alert for signs of trouble.

%% u0021:0-40 %%
Delwath: You turn down the music a tiny bit then.

%% u0022:0-13, u0023:0-214, u0024:0-86 %%
DM: Yeah we zoom, to the scout riding slightly ahead, just as a loud roar can be heard some distance to the south, on the road followed quickly by the sounds of battle. She signals to her companions and they turn out to investigate. Now, our five adventurers ride hard towards danger, alert for any signs of the dragon.

%% u0025:0-130, u0026:0-166, u0027:0-128, u0028:0-131, u0029:0-77, u0030:0-104 %%
DM: In the lead rides the scout Marcella, a short agile woman with unkempt brown hair mostly hidden under her stained traveling cloak. Just beside her rides Hektor, a big burly man wearing only a cloak and a stained sailor's tunic, his halberd drawn and ready as he rides towards the sounds of combat. Following are two men: Kadmos, leader of the expedition, tall with an air of mystery, little of his face visible under his dark—and Casian, short and full of nervous energy, constantly glancing about, short hair close cropped, wearing stained white traveling. Watching the rear rides Antonia, a red-haired woman in a heavy suit of armor, that seems to be perfectly molded to fit her body and that she carries lightly, despite her light frame.

%% u0031:0-257, u0032:0-96 %%
DM: The adventurers crest the low ridge on the north side of the road to Darba and see the scene of victory and new battle below: the two dead wyverns shattered on the ground, a large group of humans, mostly in poor-fitting and badly maintained gear, two monks, as well as two dwarves, an elf and a halfling, a strange crew to be traveling on this back road.

%% u0033:0-244, u0034:0-205, u0035:0-99, u0036:0-64, u0037:0-126, u0038:0-264, u0039:0-119, u0040:0-63 %%
DM: These are the heroes of Karawa, rumored to have killed Agata, the mother, in her lair. Kadmos nods subtly to Casian; he rides forward to negotiate, hoping for a chance to redeem their so far rather disappointing attempts at antiquities hunting. As Casian speaks, asking if they will sell or barter anything of Drankorian origin, Kadmos is only half paying attention as the halfling insists on using his tricks to speak at him and it's hard to ignore. Yes, they are looking for a scepter, an item of great great danger left from the Drankorian Empire. This would indeed be a prize to bring back to the Great Library. Kadmos's attention returns to the conversation as he senses the tension rising, these travelers' refusal to treat with Casian. Casian is not always the smoothest negotiator, but things are getting out of hand. Reaching towards the well of power that always lingers near, Kadmos casts a charm and watches as one of the dwarves, the elf, the halfling relax into an attitude of easy friendship. As he nods to Casian to proceed, out of the corner of his eye, he sees one of the monks begin to rush forward in anger. These ridiculous Dunmari. Hektor will deal with him, he thinks.
